\displaystyle=a_{\eta}^{i}\left(x_{0}^{-1}\left(c\right)\triangleleft d\right)\Big[\left(\theta_{k}\otimes{\hbox{$\rm id$}}\right)\circ\Big[\mathring{\rho}\left(a_{\eta}^{j}\right)+\sum\limits_{\left(\eta\right)}a_{\eta^{\prime}}^{j}\otimes a_{\eta^{\prime\prime}}^{k}\Big]+a_{x_{k}}^{j}\otimes a_{\eta}^{k}\Big](c\otimes d).

For
x_{k}\neq x_{0}
,

\displaystyle a_{x_{k}\eta}^{j}\left(c\triangleleft d\right)

\displaystyle=\Big[\left(\theta_{k}\otimes{\hbox{$\rm id$}}\right)\circ\Big[\mathring{\rho}\left(a_{\eta}^{j}\right)+\sum\limits_{\left(\eta\right)}a_{\eta^{\prime}}^{j}\otimes a_{\eta^{\prime\prime}}^{k}\Big]+a_{x_{k}}^{j}\otimes a_{\eta}^{k}\Big](c\otimes d)

\displaystyle=a_{\eta}^{j}\left(x_{k}^{-1}\left(c\right)\triangleleft d+x_{k}^{-1}\left(c\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}\,d\right)

Hence,

\displaystyle a_{x_{0}\eta}^{i}\left(x_{0}c\triangleleft d\right)

\displaystyle=a_{\eta}^{i}\left(c\triangleleft d\right)=a_{x_{0}\eta}^{i}\left(x_{0}(c\triangleleft d)\right)

\displaystyle a_{x_{k}\eta}^{i}\left(x_{0}c\triangleleft d\right)

\displaystyle=0=a_{x_{k}\eta}^{i}\left(x_{0}\left(c\triangleleft d\right)\right)

\displaystyle a_{x_{k}\eta}^{i}\left(x_{k}c\triangleleft d\right)

\displaystyle=a_{\eta}^{i}\left(c\triangleleft d+\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d\right)\right)=a_{x_{k}\eta}^{i}\left(x_{k}\left(c\triangleleft d+\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle a_{x_{0}\eta}^{i}\left(x_{k}c\triangleleft d\right)

\displaystyle=0=a_{\eta}^{i}\left(x_{k}\left(c\triangleleft d+\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d\right)\right)\right).

Therefore, for all
c,d\in\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$}
,

\displaystyle x_{0}c\triangleleft d

\displaystyle=x_{0}\left(c\triangleleft d\right)

\displaystyle x_{k}c\triangleleft d

\displaystyle=x_{k}\left(c\triangleleft d\right)+x_{k}\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}\,d\right)\quad\quad\forall\,k=1,2,\ldots,m.

Remark
:  The product
\triangleleft
is the graded dual of linearized coaction map. We show in Section
7
, that
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},\triangleleft\right)
is a right pre-Lie algebra with additional structure.

Graded Dual Right pre-Lie Algebra on
{\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle

Since
V^{+}
is a graded right pre-Lie coalgebra, its graded dual
{\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle
inherits a graded right pre-Lie algebra structure, where pre-Lie product is denoted by
\bullet
viz

\displaystyle\mathring{\Delta}a_{\eta}^{i}\left(c\otimes d\right)=\left(c\bullet d\right)_{i}\left(\eta\right)

Theorem 6.3
.

\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},\bullet\right)
is a graded right pre-Lie algebra such that for all
c,d\in\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$}

(16)

c\bullet d=\left(c\triangleleft d\right)+\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d\right),

Proof:
This follows from Theorem
6.2
,
15
and the fact that

\displaystyle\mathring{\Delta}_{{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}}a_{\eta}^{i}\left(c\otimes d\right)=\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d\right)_{j}\left(\eta\right).

The derived Lie algebra is
\mathfrak{g}_{\bullet}:=\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},\left[\cdot,\cdot\right]_{\bullet}\right)
.

Remark
:  Let
\mathcal{U}\left(\mathfrak{g}_{\bullet}\right)
denote the universal enveloping algebra of the Lie algebra
\mathfrak{g}_{\bullet}
. Since
\mathcal{H}
is a graded commutative Hopf algebra, its graded dual, denoted by
\mathring{\mathcal{H}}
, is a cocommutative Hopf algebra. Thus, by Cartier–Milnor–Moore theorem,
\mathring{\mathcal{H}}
is isomorphic to
\mathcal{U}\left(\mathfrak{g}_{\bullet}\right)
as graded Hopf algebras.

7.
com-pre-Lie Structure on
{\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle
associated with a linear Endomorphism

The definition of Foissy’s com-pre-Lie algebra is given as follows: Let
V
be a
k
–vector space where
k
is a field of characteristic
0
.

Definition 7.1
.

[
13
]

\left(\mathcal{A},\oslash,\bullet\right)
is a (right) com-pre-Lie algebra if

(i)

\left(\mathcal{A},\oslash\right)
is an associative and commutative algebra.

(ii)

\left(\mathcal{A},\bullet\right)
is a right pre-Lie algebra.

and for all
a,b,c\in\mathcal{A}

\left(a\oslash b\right)\bullet c=\left(a\bullet c\right)\oslash b+a\oslash\left(b\bullet c\right).

Theorem 7.1
.

(1)

If
\left(\mathcal{A},\oslash,\bullet\right)
is right com-pre-Lie, then
\mathcal{A}
inherits another right pre-Lie product, denoted by
\diamond
and defined for all
a,b\in\mathcal{A}
as
a\diamond b=a\bullet b+a\oslash b
.

(2)

\left(\mathcal{A},\left[\cdot,\cdot\right]_{\diamond}\right)
is the derived Lie algebra of right pre-Lie algebra
\left(\mathcal{A},\diamond\right)
with
\left[a,b\right]_{\diamond}=\left[a,b\right]_{\bullet}
.

Proof:

(1)

Observe that
\left(\mathcal{A},\diamond\right)
is a Lie–admissible algebra. Indeed, for all
a,b,c\in\mathcal{A}

\displaystyle\left(a\diamond b\right)\diamond c-a\diamond\left(b\diamond c\right)

\displaystyle=\left(a\bullet b+a\oslash b\right)\diamond c-a\diamond\left(b\bullet c+b\diamond c\right)

\displaystyle=\left(a\bullet b\right)\bullet c+\left(a\oslash b\right)\bullet c+\left(a\bullet b\right)\oslash c+\left(a\oslash b\right)\oslash c

\displaystyle\quad-a\bullet\left(b\bullet c\right)-a\bullet\left(b\oslash c\right)-a\oslash\left(b\bullet c\right)-a\oslash\left(b\oslash c\right)

\displaystyle=\left(a\bullet b\right)\bullet c-a\bullet\left(b\bullet c\right)+\left(a\bullet c\right)\oslash b+\left(a\bullet b\right)\oslash c+a\bullet\left(b\oslash c\right).

The right–hand side is symmetric in
b
and
c
.

(2)

Follows from statement
(1)
.

Remark
:  There are two pre-Lie products,
\diamond
and
\bullet
, whose derived Lie algebras are identical and with the difference of a commutative product.

Following Foissy
[
14
]
, let
g\in{\rm End}\left({\mathbb{R}}X\right)
, where
{\mathbb{R}}X
is the
{\mathbb{R}}
-span of the alphabet
X
. A com-pre-Lie algebra on proper polynomials,
{\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle
, associated with such a
g
(under some assumption) is defined in Theorem
7.3
, whose definition generalizes the
\triangleleft
in Theorem
6.2
. By virtue of Theorem
7.1
,
{\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle
inherits another pre-Lie product, which generalizes the pre-Lie product
\bullet
defined in Theorem
6.3
.

Let
e_{j}
for
j=1,2,\ldots,m
denote the set of standard unit vectors in
{\mathbb{R}}^{m}
.

Definition 7.2
.

Define a magmatic product
\triangleleft
on the vector space
{\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle
by induction on the degree of polynomials:

\displaystyle x_{i}e_{j}\triangleleft d

\displaystyle=g(x_{i})d_{i}e_{j}

(17)

\displaystyle x_{i}c\triangleleft d

\displaystyle=x_{i}\left(c\triangleleft d\right)+g(x_{i})\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}d\right)

where
d_{0}:=0
,
x_{i}\in X
and
c,d\in\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$}
.

Theorem 7.2
.

For all
c,d,h\in\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$}
:

(i)

\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d\right)\triangleleft h=\left(c\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d+c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)
.

(ii)

\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d\right)\triangleleft h=\left(c\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d+c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)
.

Proof:

(i)

The proof is by induction on
n=\deg\left(c\right)+\deg\left(d\right)
. For base case (
n=2
), let
c=x_{i}\mathbbm{1}
and
d=x_{j}\mathbbm{1}
.

\displaystyle\left(x_{i}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}x_{j}\mathbbm{1}\right)\triangleleft h

\displaystyle=\left(x_{i}x_{j}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft h\right)+\left(x_{j}x_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft h\right)

\displaystyle=x_{i}\left(x_{j}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft h\right)+g(x_{i})\left(x_{j}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}h\right)+x_{j}\left(x_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft h\right)+g(x_{j})\left(x_{i}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}h\right)

\displaystyle=x_{i}g(x_{j})h_{j}\mathbbm{1}+g(x_{i})\left(x_{j}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}h\right)+x_{j}g(x_{i})h_{i}\mathbbm{1}+g(x_{j})\left(x_{i}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{j}h\right)

\displaystyle=\left(g(x_{i})h_{i}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}x_{j}\mathbbm{1}\right)+\left(x_{i}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}g(x_{j})h_{j}\mathbbm{1}\right)

\displaystyle=x_{i}\left(\left(c\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}x_{j}d\right)+x_{j}\left(x_{i}\left(c\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+g(x_{i})\left(\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule \displaystyle\quad x_{i}\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}x_{j}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)+x_{j}\left(x_{i}c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+x_{i}\left(g(x_{j})\left(d{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{j}h\right)\right)+g(x_{j})\left(x_{i}c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}\left(d{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule \displaystyle=\left(x_{i}c\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}x_{j}d+x_{i}c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}\left(x_{j}d\triangleleft h\right).

(ii)

Observe that
c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d=c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d_{k}\mathbbm{1}
and thus using
\left(i\right)
,

\displaystyle\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d\right)\triangleleft h

\displaystyle=\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d_{k}\mathbbm{1}\right)\triangleleft h

\displaystyle=\left(c\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d_{k}\mathbbm{1}+c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}\left(d_{k}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft h\right)

\displaystyle=\left(c\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d+c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)_{k}\mathbbm{1}

\displaystyle=\left(c\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}d+c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{k}\left(d\triangleleft h\right).

Theorem 7.3
.

\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},g,\triangleleft\right)
is a pre-Lie algebra if and only if
g
is of the form
g(x_{i})=\alpha_{i}x_{i}+\beta_{i}x_{0}
, for all
i=1,2,\ldots,m
.

Proof:
It is enough to prove that

(18)

\displaystyle\left(c\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h-c\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)

is symmetric in
d
and
h
for all
c,d,h\in\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$}
.

Assume that
g(x_{i})=\alpha_{i}x_{i}+\beta_{i}x_{0}
for
i\neq 0
and
g(x_{0})=\sum_{j}a_{0j}x_{j}
.

The proof is by induction on
n=\deg(c)
. For the base case
(n=1)
; assume
c=x_{i}\mathbbm{1}
. If
x_{i}=x_{0}
then (
18
) is trivial, otherwise

\displaystyle\left(x_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h-x_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\alpha_{i}x_{i}d_{i}\mathbbm{1}\right)\triangleleft h-\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)_{i}\mathbbm{1}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{i}\left(d_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft h\right)+\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(d_{i}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}h\right)-\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)_{i}\mathbbm{1}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(d{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}h\right)_{i}\mathbbm{1}

which is symmetric in
h
and
d
. Assume that the result is true for
n\leq N
; let
c=x_{i}c^{\prime}
where
\deg(c^{\prime})=N
. If
x_{i}=x_{0}

\displaystyle\left(x_{0}c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h-x_{0}c^{\prime}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)=x_{0}\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h-x_{0}\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)=0.

For the case
x_{i}\neq x_{0}
,

\displaystyle\left(x_{i}c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h-x_{i}c^{\prime}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)

\displaystyle=\left(x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right)+\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}d\right)\right)\triangleleft h-\left(x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)-\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h+\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}d\right)\triangleleft h-x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)-\alpha x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)

\displaystyle=x_{i}\left(\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h\right)+\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule h\right)+\alpha_{i}^{2}x_{i}\left(\left(c^{\prime}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule \displaystyle\quad\quad-x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)-\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)

\displaystyle=x_{i}\left(\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h-c^{\prime}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)\right)+\alpha_{i}^{2}x_{i}\left(c^{\prime}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}\left(d{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}h\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft h\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}d\right)+\alpha_{i}x_{i}\left(\left(c^{\prime}\triangleleft d\right){\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{i}h\right)\quad\text{(c.f.~Theorem~ \ref{th:diff-over-sh})}.

is symmetric in
d
and
h
.

Suppose
g(x_{i})=a_{i0}x_{0}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}a_{ij}x_{j}
for
i,j=1,2,\ldots,m
such that
\exists\,a_{ij}\neq 0
for some
i\neq j
.

\displaystyle\left(x_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft d\right)\triangleleft h-x_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft\left(d\triangleleft h\right)=\left(g(x_{i})d_{i}\mathbbm{1}\right)\triangleleft h-g(x_{i})\left(d\triangleleft h\right)_{i}\mathbbm{1}

\displaystyle=g(x_{i})\left(d_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft h\right)-g(x_{i})\left(d_{i}\mathbbm{1}\triangleleft h\right)+a_{i0}g(x_{0})\cancel{\left(d_{i}\mathbbm{{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}}h_{0}\right)}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}a_{ij}g(x_{j})\left(d_{i}\mathbbm{1}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}_{j}h\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}a_{ij}g(x_{j})\left(d_{i}{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}h_{j}\right)\mathbbm{1}

The right-hand is symmetric in
d
and
h
only if
i=j
.

With
X=\{x_{0},x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{m}\}
in natural order, the matrix representation of the endomorphism
g
, denoted by
\left[g\right]_{X}
for which
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},g,\triangleleft\right)
becomes a right pre-Lie algebra is

(19)

\displaystyle\left[g\right]_{X}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}a_{00}&a_{01}&a_{02}&a_{03}&\dots&a_{0m}\\
a_{10}&a_{11}&0&0&\ldots&0\\
a_{20}&0&a_{22}&0&\ldots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
a_{m0}&0&0&0&\ldots&a_{mm}\end{pmatrix}

where the non–zero (not necessarily zero) elements
a_{ij}\in{\mathbb{R}}
. The submatrix of
\left[g\right]_{X}
when restricted to
X\setminus\{x_{0}\}
is a diagonal matrix.

The following theorem is a result of Theorem
7.2
and Theorem
7.3
.

Theorem 7.4
.

For
g\in{\rm End}\left({\mathbb{R}}X\right)
whose matrix representation is of the form in (
19
),

(i)

\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},g,{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,},\triangleleft\right)
is a right com-pre-Lie algebra. Thus,
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},\bullet\right)
is a right pre-Lie algebra where
c\bullet d=\left(c\triangleleft d\right)+\left(c{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,}d\right)
for all
c,d\in\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$}
.

(ii)

The derived Lie algebras of both right pre-Lie algebras
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},g,\triangleleft\right)
and
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},\bullet\right)
are identical.

7.1.
pre-Lie in Multiplicative Feedback: A Special Case

The product
\triangleleft
on
{\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle
, defined in Theorem
6.2
obtained as graded dual of linearized coaction map
\mathring{\rho}
is a special case of
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},g,\triangleleft\right)
with block matrix representation of
g
as:

\displaystyle\left[g\right]_{X}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\begin{matrix}0\end{matrix}&\hskip-5.0pt\vline\hskip-5.0pt&\begin{matrix}0&\ldots&0\end{matrix}\\
\hline\cr\begin{matrix}0\\
\vdots\\
0\end{matrix}&\hskip-5.0pt\vline\hskip-5.0pt&\LARGE{\mathcal{I}_{m}}\end{pmatrix}

where
\mathcal{I}_{m}
is the
m\times m
identity matrix.

The following result is a thus a consequence of Theorem
7.4
.

Theorem 7.5
.

(1)

The product
\triangleleft:\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$}^{\otimes 2}\longrightarrow\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$}
, defined in Theorem
6.2
is (right) pre-Lie.

(2)

\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},{\,{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 3.5pt\vrule height=5.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}{\mathbin{\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt}\hrule}\vbox{\hbox{\vrule\hskip 2.6pt\vrule height=3.0pt,width=0.0pt\vrule}\hrule}}}}}\,},\triangleleft\right)
is a com-pre-Lie algebra.

Remark
:  Thus, the Hopf algebra
\mathcal{H}
can be reconstructed by taking the graded dual of universal enveloping Hopf algebra of the right pre-Lie algebra
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}^{m}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},\triangleleft\right)
obtained via Guin–Oudum construction
[
23
]
.

m=1
: A Special Case

For
m=1
, then
X=\{x_{0},x_{1}\}
, then
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},g,\triangleleft\right)
is a right com-pre-Lie algebra for any linear endomorphism
g
as from
(\ref{eq:matrix-form-g})
,

\displaystyle\left[g\right]_{X}=\begin{pmatrix}a_{00}&a_{01}\\
a_{10}&a_{11}\end{pmatrix}

which are all arbitrary elements
a_{ij}\in{\mathbb{R}}
. For more details on the com-pre-Lie algebra
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},g,\triangleleft\right)
, we refer to Foissy
[
14
]
. As a particular example, for the nilpotent matrix

\displaystyle\left[g\right]_{X}=\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}

we obtain the com-pre-Lie (sub)algebra
\left(\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}_{p}\langle X\rangle$},g,\triangleleft\right)
corresponding to SISO (single–input, single–output) additive feedback interconnection. It is a com-pre-Lie subalgebra as, the pre-Lie–algebra corresponding to additive feedback is defined on the space of
\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}\langle X\rangle$}{}\subseteq\mbox{${\mathbb{R}}\langle\langle X\rangle\rangle$}
(not just
{\mathbb{R}}_{p}\langle X\rangle
). For more details, we refer to Foissy
[
13
]
.
